Introducing collaborative signals through retrieval paths is a good choice, but most of the existing retrieval path collection schemes use the existing Explainable GNN algorithms. Although these methods are effective, they are relatively unexplainable and not be suitable for the recommendation field.
  To address the above challenges, we propose MMP-Refer, a framework using \textbf{M}ulti\textbf{M}odal Retrieval \textbf{P}aths with \textbf{Re}trieval-augmented LLM \textbf{F}or \textbf{E}xplainable \textbf{R}ecommendation. We use a sequential recommendation model based on joint residual coding to obtain multimodal embeddings, and design a heuristic search algorithm to obtain retrieval paths by multimodal embeddings; In the generation phase, we integrated a trainable lightweight collaborative adapter to map the graph encoding of interaction subgraphs to the semantic space of the LLM, as soft prompts to enhance the understanding of interaction information by the LLM. Extensive experiments have demonstrated the effectiveness of our approach. Codes and data are available at https://github.com/pxcstart/MMP-Refer.

</details>


### [77] [Are LLM-Based Retrievers Worth Their Cost? An Empirical Study of Efficiency, Robustness, and Reasoning Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03676)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,Jamie Holdcroft,Mohammed Ali,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文系统评估了大型语言模型检索器在复杂查询中的性能，包括效率、鲁棒性、置信度校准和推理开销等多个维度，发现推理增强型检索器在保持吞吐量的同时具有较强效果，但置信度校准普遍较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型检索器在复杂查询上表现优异，但其实际应用价值不仅取决于准确性，还需要考虑效率、鲁棒性和可靠的置信度信号。现有评估主要关注准确性，缺乏对这些关键实用维度的全面评估。

Method: 在12个任务和14个检索器上复现了推理密集型检索基准(BRIGHT)，并扩展评估维度：冷启动索引成本、查询延迟分布和吞吐量、语料库扩展、对受控查询扰动的鲁棒性、置信度使用(AUROC)预测查询成功率。量化推理开销，比较标准查询与五个推理增强变体，测量准确性增益相对于增加的延迟。

Result: 1) 某些推理专用检索器在保持吞吐量竞争力的同时实现强效性，而几个基于大型LLM的双编码器则因适度增益而承受显著延迟；2) 推理增强对小于1B参数的编码器产生最小延迟，但对顶级检索器显示收益递减，在正式数学/代码领域可能降低性能；3) 置信度校准在不同模型家族中普遍较弱，原始检索分数在下游路由中不可靠。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型检索器的实际部署需要超越准确性指标的全面评估。推理增强在效率与效果间存在权衡，置信度校准是当前系统的薄弱环节。研究提供了可复现的代码和工具，为检索系统的实用评估建立了基准。

Abstract: Large language model retrievers improve performance on complex queries, but their practical value depends on efficiency, robustness, and reliable confidence signals in addition to accuracy. We reproduce a reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmark (BRIGHT) across 12 tasks and 14 retrievers, and extend evaluation with cold-start indexing cost, query latency distributions and throughput, corpus scaling, robustness to controlled query perturbations, and confidence use (AUROC) for predicting query success. We also quantify \emph{reasoning overhead} by comparing standard queries to five provided reasoning-augmented variants, measuring accuracy gains relative to added latency. We find that some reasoning-specialized retrievers achieve strong effectiveness while remaining competitive in throughput, whereas several large LLM-based bi-encoders incur substantial latency for modest gains. Reasoning augmentation incurs minimal latency for sub-1B encoders but exhibits diminishing returns for top retrievers and may reduce performance on formal math/code domains. Confidence calibration is consistently weak across model families, indicating that raw retrieval scores are unreliable for downstream routing without additional calibration. We release all code and artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [78] [Fusion and Alignment Enhancement with Large Language Models for Tail-item Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03688)
*Zhifu Wei,Yizhou Dang,Guibing Guo,Chuang Zhao,Zhu Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FAERec框架通过自适应门控机制融合ID和LLM嵌入，采用双重对齐解决结构不一致问题，提升尾部物品序列推荐性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决序列推荐中的尾部物品问题，现有方法在融合协同信号与语义知识方面效果不佳，且忽视ID与LLM嵌入空间的结构不一致性

Method: 提出FAERec框架：1) 自适应门控机制动态融合ID和LLM嵌入；2) 双重对齐方法：物品级对齐通过对比学习建立对应关系，特征级对齐约束维度间相关性模式；3) 课程学习调度器调整对齐权重

Result: 在三个广泛使用的数据集上，与多个代表性SR骨干模型进行实验，证明了框架的有效性和泛化能力

Conclusion: FAERec通过融合和增强对齐机制，有效解决了尾部物品序列推荐问题，提升了物品表示质量和推荐准确性

Abstract: Sequential Recommendation (SR) learns user preferences from their historical interaction sequences and provides personalized suggestions. In real-world scenarios, most items exhibit sparse interactions, known as the tail-item problem. This issue limits the model's ability to accurately capture item transition patterns. To tackle this, large language models (LLMs) offer a promising solution by capturing semantic relationships between items. Despite previous efforts to leverage LLM-derived embeddings for enriching tail items, they still face the following limitations: 1) They struggle to effectively fuse collaborative signals with semantic knowledge, leading to suboptimal item embedding quality. 2) Existing methods overlook the structural inconsistency between the ID and LLM embedding spaces, causing conflicting signals that degrade recommendation accuracy. In this work, we propose a Fusion and Alignment Enhancement framework with LLMs for Tail-item Sequential Recommendation (FAERec), which improves item representations by generating coherently-fused and structurally consistent embeddings. For the information fusion challenge, we design an adaptive gating mechanism that dynamically fuses ID and LLM embeddings. Then, we propose a dual-level alignment approach to mitigate structural inconsistency. The item-level alignment establishes correspondences between ID and LLM embeddings of the same item through contrastive learning, while the feature-level alignment constrains the correlation patterns between corresponding dimensions across the two embedding spaces. Furthermore, the weights of the two alignments are adjusted by a curriculum learning scheduler to avoid premature optimization of the complex feature-level objective. Extensive experiments across three widely used datasets with multiple representative SR backbones demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our framework.

</details>


### [79] [Rank, Don't Generate: Statement-level Ranking for Explainable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03724)
*Ben Kabongo,Arthur Satouf,Vincent Guigue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出将可解释推荐重新定义为陈述级排序问题而非生成问题，通过从评论中提取候选解释性陈述并排序返回前k个，以减少幻觉并支持标准化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前使用大语言模型生成文本解释来证明推荐合理性，但评估这些解释仍面临重大挑战。需要一种能够减轻幻觉、支持细粒度事实分析、并实现标准化可重复评估的方法。

Method: 1) 将可解释推荐形式化为陈述级排序问题；2) 使用基于LLM的提取管道从评论中生成解释性和原子性陈述；3) 采用可扩展的语义聚类方法合并同义表达以确保唯一性；4) 构建StaR基准数据集，在全局级和物品级两个层面评估排序模型。

Result: 基于流行度的基线方法在全局级排序中表现有竞争力，但在物品级排序中平均优于最先进模型，揭示了在个性化解释排序方面的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 陈述排序方法通过构造减轻了幻觉，支持细粒度事实分析，并实现了标准化评估。研究结果表明当前个性化解释排序模型存在不足，需要进一步改进以更好地处理物品特定的解释排序。

Abstract: Textual explanations, generated with large language models (LLMs), are increasingly used to justify recommendations. Yet, evaluating these explanations remains a critical challenge. We advocate a shift in objective: rank, don't generate. We formalize explainable recommendation as a statement-level ranking problem, where systems rank candidate explanatory statements derived from reviews and return the top-k as explanation. This formulation mitigates hallucination by construction and enables fine-grained factual analysis. It also models factor importance through relevance scores and supports standardized, reproducible evaluation with established ranking metrics. Meaningful assessment, however, requires each statement to be explanatory (item facts affecting user experience), atomic (one opinion about one aspect), and unique (paraphrases consolidated), which is challenging to obtain from noisy reviews. We address this with (i) an LLM-based extraction pipeline producing explanatory and atomic statements, and (ii) a scalable, semantic clustering method consolidating paraphrases to enforce uniqueness. Building on this pipeline, we introduce StaR, a benchmark for statement ranking in explainable recommendation, constructed from four Amazon Reviews 2014 product categories. We evaluate popularity-based baselines and state-of-the-art models under global-level (all statements) and item-level (target item statements) ranking. Popularity baselines are competitive in global-level ranking but outperform state-of-the-art models on average in item-level ranking, exposing critical limitations in personalized explanation ranking.

</details>


### [80] [Semantic IDs for Recommender Systems at Snapchat: Use Cases, Technical Challenges, and Design Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03949)
*Clark Mingxuan Ju,Tong Zhao,Leonardo Neves,Liam Collins,Bhuvesh Kumar,Jiwen Ren,Lili Zhang,Wenfeng Zhuo,Vincent Zhang,Xiao Bai,Jinchao Li,Karthik Iyer,Zihao Fan,Yilun Xu,Yiwen Chen,Peicheng Yu,Manish Malik,Neil Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了在Snapchat推荐系统中应用语义ID（SIDs）作为辅助特征和检索源的实践经验，包括技术挑战、实验设计和生产部署效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统原子ID虽然能编码协同过滤信号，但存在基数大、缺乏语义结构的问题。语义ID（SIDs）通过有序代码列表表示项目，具有更小的基数并能诱导语义聚类，有望提升推荐系统的效果。

Method: 采用残差量化等分词器从基础模型或协同信号中提取语义表示，生成有序代码列表作为语义ID。将SIDs作为排序模型的辅助特征，并探索其作为不同ML应用中的额外检索源。通过实验迭代设计选择，解决实际应用中的技术挑战。

Result: 在内部数据和学术基准测试中获得了有前景的离线结果，通过在线A/B测试验证了SID变体在多个生产模型中的有效性，取得了积极的指标影响。

Conclusion: 语义ID（SIDs）作为推荐系统中的有效项目标识符，在实际应用中展现出显著优势，已成功部署于Snapchat的多个生产模型，证明了其作为辅助特征和检索源的实用价值。

Abstract: Effective item identifiers (IDs) are an important component for recommender systems (RecSys) in practice, and are commonly adopted in many use cases such as retrieval and ranking. IDs can encode collaborative filtering signals within training data, such that RecSys models can extrapolate during the inference and personalize the prediction based on users' behavioral histories. Recently, Semantic IDs (SIDs) have become a trending paradigm for RecSys. In comparison to the conventional atomic ID, an SID is an ordered list of codes, derived from tokenizers such as residual quantization, applied to semantic representations commonly extracted from foundation models or collaborative signals. SIDs have drastically smaller cardinality than the atomic counterpart, and induce semantic clustering in the ID space. At Snapchat, we apply SIDs as auxiliary features for ranking models, and also explore SIDs as additional retrieval sources in different ML applications. In this paper, we discuss practical technical challenges we encountered while applying SIDs, experiments we have conducted, and design choices we have iterated to mitigate these challenges. Backed by promising offline results on both internal data and academic benchmarks as well as online A/B studies, SID variants have been launched in multiple production models with positive metrics impact.

</details>


### [81] [MisEdu-RAG: A Misconception-Aware Dual-Hypergraph RAG for Novice Math Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04036)
*Zhihan Guo,Rundong Xue,Yuting Lu,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MisEdu-RAG：基于双超图的检索增强生成框架，用于数学教师处理学生错误概念，通过概念超图和实例超图组织教学知识，提供可操作的诊断和教学建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新手数学教师难以诊断和纠正学生错误概念，现有LLM平台将教学知识与学生错误松散结合，导致指导不够具体可操作。需要一种能更好连接教学原理与实际错误案例的系统。

Method: 提出MisEdu-RAG双超图RAG框架：1)概念超图组织教学知识，2)实例超图组织真实学生错误案例。采用两阶段检索：从两个超图层级收集相关证据，基于检索到的案例和教学原则生成响应。

Result: 在MisstepMath数据集上评估：相比基线模型，token-F1提升10.95%，五维度响应质量最高提升15.3%，在多样性和赋能维度提升最大。221名教师问卷和6名新手访谈表明，系统能为高需求错误概念场景提供诊断结果和具体教学策略。

Conclusion: MisEdu-RAG展示了在处理错误概念方面用于可扩展教师培训和AI辅助教学的强大潜力，能提供基于教学原则和实际案例的具体指导。

Abstract: Novice math teachers often encounter students' mistakes that are difficult to diagnose and remediate. Misconceptions are especially challenging because teachers must explain what went wrong and how to solve them. Although many existing large language model (LLM) platforms can assist in generating instructional feedback, these LLMs loosely connect pedagogical knowledge and student mistakes, which might make the guidance less actionable for teachers. To address this gap, we propose MisEdu-RAG, a dual-hypergraph-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that organizes pedagogical knowledge as a concept hypergraph and real student mistake cases as an instance hypergraph. Given a query, MisEdu-RAG performs a two-stage retrieval to gather connected evidence from both layers and generates a response grounded in the retrieved cases and pedagogical principles. We evaluate on \textit{MisstepMath}, a dataset of math mistakes paired with teacher solutions, as a benchmark for misconception-aware retrieval and response generation across topics and error types. Evaluation results on \textit{MisstepMath} show that, compared with baseline models, MisEdu-RAG improves token-F1 by 10.95\% and yields up to 15.3\% higher five-dimension response quality, with the largest gains on \textit{Diversity} and \textit{Empowerment}. To verify its applicability in practical use, we further conduct a pilot study through a questionnaire survey of 221 teachers and interviews with 6 novices. The findings suggest that MisEdu-RAG provides diagnosis results and concrete teaching moves for high-demand misconception scenarios. Overall, MisEdu-RAG demonstrates strong potential for scalable teacher training and AI-assisted instruction for misconception handling. Our code is available on GitHub: https://github.com/GEMLab-HKU/MisEdu-RAG.

</details>


### [82] [Formalized Information Needs Improve Large-Language-Model Relevance Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04140)
*Jüri Keller,Maik Fröbe,Björn Engelmann,Fabian Haak,Timo Breuer,Birger Larsen,Philipp Schaer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LLM作为相关性评估者时，使用形式化的信息需求（主题）能提高评估可靠性，减少误判并提升与人工评估的一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Cranfield式检索评估中，相关文档数量过多或过少以及评估者间一致性低会降低观察可靠性。人工评估通常使用形式化的检索主题来避免这些问题，但新兴的LLM评估方法往往仅使用查询，可能降低可靠性。研究旨在探索LLM相关性评估者是否受益于形式化的信息需求。

Method: 使用LLM将信息需求合成形式化为遵循先前人工相关性评估结构的主题（包括描述和叙述）。在Robust04和TREC Deep Learning 2019/2020数据集上，比较使用合成形式化主题的评估者与仅使用查询的默认LLM评估者。分析相关文档数量、评估者间一致性以及与人工评估的一致性。

Result: 未形式化的评估者判断更多文档为相关且一致性较低，导致检索评估可靠性下降。形式化主题提高了人工与LLM相关性判断之间的一致性，即使这些主题与人工创建的主题相似度不高。形式化减少了误判并提升了评估可靠性。

Conclusion: LLM相关性评估者应像人工评估一样使用形式化的信息需求。当不存在人工形式化时，应使用LLM合成形式化主题以提高评估可靠性。形式化信息需求对LLM评估同样重要，能减少误判并提升与人工评估的一致性。

Abstract: Cranfield-style retrieval evaluations with too few or too many relevant documents or with low inter-assessor agreement on relevance can reduce the reliability of observations. In evaluations with human assessors, information needs are often formalized as retrieval topics to avoid an excessive number of relevant documents while maintaining good agreement. However, emerging evaluation setups that use Large Language Models (LLMs) as relevance assessors often use only queries, potentially decreasing the reliability. To study whether LLM relevance assessors benefit from formalized information needs, we synthetically formalize information needs with LLMs into topics that follow the established structure from previous human relevance assessments (i.e., descriptions and narratives). We compare assessors using synthetically formalized topics against the LLM-default query-only assessor on Robust04 and the 2019/2020 editions of TREC Deep Learning. We find that assessors without formalization judge many more documents relevant and have a lower agreement, leading to reduced reliability in retrieval evaluations. Furthermore, we show that the formalized topics improve agreement between human and LLM relevance judgments, even when the topics are not highly similar to their human counterparts. Our findings indicate that LLM relevance assessors should use formalized information needs, as is standard for human assessment, and synthetically formalize topics when no human formalization exists to improve evaluation reliability.

</details>


### [83] [A Logical-Rule Autoencoder for Interpretable Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04270)
*Jinhao Pan,Bowen Wei,Ziwei Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出逻辑规则可解释自编码器（LIA），通过可学习的逻辑规则层实现协同过滤的可解释性，在保持高性能的同时提供人类可读的推荐决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数深度学习推荐模型作为黑箱运行，依赖潜在表示而掩盖决策过程，缺乏内在可解释性，这在需要透明度和问责制的应用中引发担忧。

Method: 提出逻辑规则可解释自编码器（LIA），引入可学习的逻辑规则层，每个规则神经元配备门参数，在训练期间自动选择AND和OR运算符；通过连接权重的符号编码否定，实现参数高效的功能完备性；学习显式的人类可读重构规则。

Result: 广泛实验表明，该方法在保持完全可解释性的同时，实现了比传统基线更好的推荐性能。

Conclusion: LIA通过设计实现可解释性，允许用户直接追踪每个推荐背后的决策过程，为需要透明度和问责制的推荐应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Most deep learning recommendation models operate as black boxes, relying on latent representations that obscure their decision process. This lack of intrinsic interpretability raises concerns in applications that require transparency and accountability. In this work, we propose a Logical-rule Interpretable Autoencoder (LIA) for collaborative filtering that is interpretable by design. LIA introduces a learnable logical rule layer in which each rule neuron is equipped with a gate parameter that automatically selects between AND and OR operators during training, enabling the model to discover diverse logical patterns directly from data. To support functional completeness without doubling the input dimensionality, LIA encodes negation through the sign of connection weights, providing a parameter-efficient mechanism for expressing both positive and negated item conditions within each rule. By learning explicit, human-readable reconstruction rules, LIA allows users to directly trace the decision process behind each recommendation. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves improved recommendation performance over traditional baselines while remaining fully interpretable. Code and data are available at https://github.com/weibowen555/LIA.

</details>


### [84] [FAVE: Flow-based Average Velocity Establishment for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04427)
*Ke Shi,Yao Zhang,Feng Guo,Jinyuan Zhang,JunShuo Zhang,Shen Gao,Shuo Shang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Fave框架通过语义锚定先验和全局平均速度学习，实现一步生成推荐，解决流式方法中的先验不匹配和线性冗余问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流的生成推荐方法受限于"噪声到数据"范式，存在两个关键低效问题：先验不匹配（从无信息噪声开始生成，需要漫长恢复轨迹）和线性冗余（迭代求解器在建模确定性偏好转移时浪费计算资源）。

Method: 提出Fave框架，采用渐进式两阶段训练策略：阶段1通过双端语义对齐建立稳定偏好空间；阶段2引入语义锚定先验（从用户交互历史获取掩码嵌入作为信息起点），学习全局平均速度将多步轨迹压缩为单一位移向量，并通过JVP一致性约束确保轨迹直线性。

Result: 在三个基准测试上的实验表明，Fave不仅达到最先进的推荐性能，而且在推理效率上实现数量级提升，适用于延迟敏感场景。

Conclusion: Fave框架通过直接学习从信息先验到目标分布的轨迹，有效解决了流式生成推荐中的效率瓶颈，实现了高效的一步生成推荐。

Abstract: Generative recommendation has emerged as a transformative paradigm for capturing the dynamic evolution of user intents in sequential recommendation. While flow-based methods improve the efficiency of diffusion models, they remain hindered by the ``Noise-to-Data'' paradigm, which introduces two critical inefficiencies: prior mismatch, where generation starts from uninformative noise, forcing a lengthy recovery trajectory; and linear redundancy, where iterative solvers waste computation on modeling deterministic preference transitions. To address these limitations, we propose a Flow-based Average Velocity Establishment (Fave) framework for one-step generation recommendation that learns a direct trajectory from an informative prior to the target distribution. Fave is structured via a progressive two-stage training strategy. In Stage 1, we establish a stable preference space through dual-end semantic alignment, applying constraints at both the source (user history) and target (next item) to prevent representation collapse. In Stage 2, we directly resolve the efficiency bottlenecks by introducing a semantic anchor prior, which initializes the flow with a masked embedding from the user's interaction history, providing an informative starting point. Then we learn a global average velocity, consolidating the multi-step trajectory into a single displacement vector, and enforce trajectory straightness via a JVP-based consistency constraint to ensure one-step generation. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that Fave not only achieves state-of-the-art recommendation performance but also delivers an order-of-magnitude improvement in inference efficiency, making it practical for latency-sensitive scenarios.

</details>


### [85] [Retrieval Augmented Conversational Recommendation with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04457)
*Zhenrui Yue,Honglei Zhuang,Zhen Qin,Zhankui He,Huimin Zeng,Julian McAuley,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RAR：首个两阶段检索增强对话推荐框架，通过动态桥接检索与生成，利用强化学习优化检索器，在统一电影语料库上实现性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的对话推荐系统依赖预训练知识，缺乏外部检索机制处理新项目，且缺少统一语料库支持检索增强。需要解决检索与生成阶段的对齐问题，并减少LLM的幻觉现象。

Method: 提出两阶段检索增强对话推荐框架RAR：1)检索器基于用户历史生成候选项目；2)LLM结合对话上下文和检索结果精炼推荐。创新性地引入强化学习方法，利用LLM反馈迭代更新检索器，通过协作反馈循环增强候选集排序。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，RAR持续优于最先进的基线方法。通过将LLM基于事实元数据，能够捕捉细微用户意图，生成上下文感知推荐并减少幻觉。

Conclusion: RAR是首个动态桥接检索与生成的两阶段对话推荐框架，通过强化学习驱动的检索器优化和统一语料库支持，显著提升了推荐性能和事实性，为LLM在对话推荐中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit enhanced capabilities in language understanding and generation. By utilizing their embedded knowledge, LLMs are increasingly used as conversational recommender systems (CRS), achieving improved performance across diverse scenarios. However, existing LLM-based methods rely on pretrained knowledge without external retrieval mechanisms for novel items. Additionally, the lack of a unified corpus poses challenges for integrating retrieval augmentation into CRS. Motivated by these challenges, we present RAR, a novel two-stage retrieval augmented conversational recommendation framework that aligns retrieval and generation to enhance both performance and factuality. To support this framework and provide a unified corpus, we construct a large-scale movie corpus, comprising over 300k movies with rich metadata, such as titles, casts and plot summaries. Leveraging this data, our primary contribution is RAR, the first framework to departs from standard two-stage CRS by dynamically bridging retrieval and generation. First, a retriever model generates candidate items based on user history; in the subsequent stage, an LLM refines the recommendations by incorporating conversational context with retrieved results. In addition, we introduce a novel reinforcement learning (RL) method that leverages LLM feedback to iteratively update the retriever. By creating a collaborative feedback loop that reinforces sampled candidate sets with higher ranking metrics, RAR effectively mitigates the misalignment between the retrieval and generation stages. Furthermore, grounding the LLM in factual metadata allows our RL-driven approach to capture subtle user intentions and generate context-aware recommendations with reduced hallucinations. We validate our approach through extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks, where RAR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [86] [Ruling Out to Rule In: Contrastive Hypothesis Retrieval for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04593)
*Byeolhee Kim,Min-Kyung Kim,Young-Hak Kim,Tae-Joon Jeon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CHR通过生成目标假设和模仿假设，在医学RAG中显式建模需要避免的内容，显著减少硬负例污染，提升检索质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统医学RAG检索器常检索出语义相似但临床不同的硬负例，现有查询扩展方法缺乏显式机制来抑制临床上合理的模仿病例，导致系统容易检索到掩盖实际诊断的模仿病例

Method: 提出对比假设检索（CHR）框架，受临床鉴别诊断过程启发：1）生成目标假设H+（可能正确答案）和模仿假设H-（最可能的错误替代方案）；2）通过促进H+对齐证据同时惩罚H-对齐内容来评分文档

Result: 在三个医学QA基准和三个答案生成器上，CHR在所有配置中均优于五个基线方法，相对次优方法提升高达10.4个百分点。在587个CHR正确而基线错误的案例中，85.2%的案例在top-5检索列表中无共享文档，表明实质性检索重定向而非轻微重排序

Conclusion: 通过显式建模需要避免的内容和需要寻找的内容，CHR将临床推理与检索机制设计相结合，为减少医学RAG系统中的硬负例污染提供了实用路径

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds large language models in external medical knowledge, yet standard retrievers frequently surface hard negatives that are semantically close to the query but describe clinically distinct conditions. While existing query-expansion methods improve query representation to mitigate ambiguity, they typically focus on enriching target-relevant semantics without an explicit mechanism to selectively suppress specific, clinically plausible hard negatives. This leaves the system prone to retrieving plausible mimics that overshadow the actual diagnosis, particularly when such mimics are dominant within the corpus. We propose Contrastive Hypothesis Retrieval (CHR), a framework inspired by the process of clinical differential diagnosis. CHR generates a target hypothesis $H^+$ for the likely correct answer and a mimic hypothesis $H^-$ for the most plausible incorrect alternative, then scores documents by promoting $H^+$-aligned evidence while penalizing $H^-$-aligned content. Across three medical QA benchmarks and three answer generators, CHR outperforms all five baselines in every configuration, with improvements of up to 10.4 percentage points over the next-best method. On the $n=587$ pooled cases where CHR answers correctly while embedded hypothetical-document query expansion does not, 85.2\% have no shared documents between the top-5 retrieval lists of CHR and of that baseline, consistent with substantive retrieval redirection rather than light re-ranking of the same candidates. By explicitly modeling what to avoid alongside what to find, CHR bridges clinical reasoning with retrieval mechanism design and offers a practical path to reducing hard-negative contamination in medical RAG systems.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond Hard Negatives: The Importance of Score Distribution in Knowledge Distillation for Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04734)
*Youngjoon Jang,Seongtae Hong,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出分层采样策略，通过均匀覆盖教师模型整个分数谱来改进检索模型的知识蒸馏，优于传统硬负例挖掘方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法主要关注挖掘硬负例以提高区分度，但忽略了训练数据的系统性组成和教师分数分布。仅关注硬负例会阻碍学生模型学习教师全面的偏好结构，影响泛化能力。

Method: 提出分层采样策略，均匀覆盖教师模型的整个分数谱，保留教师分数的方差和熵，确保学生模型能学习教师对查询-文档对的全面偏好结构。

Result: 在领域内和领域外基准测试中，分层采样作为鲁棒基线显著优于top-K采样和随机采样，在多样化设置中表现更好。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏的本质在于保留教师模型感知的相对分数的多样性范围，分层采样通过全面覆盖教师分数分布有效实现了这一目标。

Abstract: Transferring knowledge from a cross-encoder teacher via Knowledge Distillation (KD) has become a standard paradigm for training retrieval models. While existing studies have largely focused on mining hard negatives to improve discrimination, the systematic composition of training data and the resulting teacher score distribution have received relatively less attention. In this work, we highlight that focusing solely on hard negatives prevents the student from learning the comprehensive preference structure of the teacher, potentially hampering generalization. To effectively emulate the teacher score distribution, we propose a Stratified Sampling strategy that uniformly covers the entire score spectrum. Experiments on in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks confirm that Stratified Sampling, which preserves the variance and entropy of teacher scores, serves as a robust baseline, significantly outperforming top-K and random sampling in diverse settings. These findings suggest that the essence of distillation lies in preserving the diverse range of relative scores perceived by the teacher.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [88] [IC3-Evolve: Proof-/Witness-Gated Offline LLM-Driven Heuristic Evolution for IC3 Hardware Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03232)
*Mingkai Miao,Guangyu Hu,Ziyi Yang,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IC3-Evolve：基于LLM的离线代码进化框架，自动优化IC3硬件模型检查算法的启发式策略，通过证明/见证门控验证确保正确性，最终生成无需ML依赖的独立检查器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC3（属性导向可达性）算法在硬件安全模型检查中广泛应用，但其性能严重依赖于大量相互作用的启发式策略和实现选择。手动调优成本高、脆弱且难以复现，需要自动化方法来可靠改进IC3实现。

Method: 提出IC3-Evolve框架：利用LLM离线生成针对IC3实现的小型、槽位受限且可审计的补丁。采用证明/见证门控验证机制：SAFE运行必须生成可独立验证的证书，UNSAFE运行必须生成可重放的计数器示例轨迹，防止不正确的编辑被部署。最终生成无ML/LLM推理开销的独立进化检查器。

Result: 在公开硬件模型检查竞赛（HWMCC）基准上进行进化，并在未见过的公开和工业模型检查基准上评估泛化能力。结果表明IC3-Evolve能够在严格正确性门控下可靠发现实用的启发式改进。

Conclusion: IC3-Evolve成功实现了IC3算法的自动化离线优化，通过LLM生成补丁并采用严格的正确性验证机制，最终产生无需运行时ML依赖的高性能独立检查器，为硬件模型检查提供了可靠的启发式改进方法。

Abstract: IC3, also known as property-directed reachability (PDR), is a commonly-used algorithm for hardware safety model checking. It checks if a state transition system complies with a given safety property. IC3 either returns UNSAFE (indicating property violation) with a counterexample trace, or SAFE with a checkable inductive invariant as the proof to safety. In practice, the performance of IC3 is dominated by a large web of interacting heuristics and implementation choices, making manual tuning costly, brittle, and hard to reproduce. This paper presents IC3-Evolve, an automated offline code-evolution framework that utilizes an LLM to propose small, slot-restricted and auditable patches to an IC3 implementation. Crucially, every candidate patch is admitted only through proof- /witness-gated validation: SAFE runs must emit a certificate that is independently checked, and UNSAFE runs must emit a replayable counterexample trace, preventing unsound edits from being deployed. Since the LLM is used only offline, the deployed artifact is a standalone evolved checker with zero ML/LLM inference overhead and no runtime model dependency. We evolve on the public hardware model checking competition (HWMCC) benchmark and evaluate the generalizability on unseen public and industrial model checking benchmarks, showing that IC3-Evolve can reliably discover practical heuristic improvements under strict correctness gates.

</details>


### [89] [Structural Segmentation of the Minimum Set Cover Problem: Exploiting Universe Decomposability for Metaheuristic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03234)
*Isidora Hernández,Héctor Ferrada,Cristóbal A. Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了最小集合覆盖问题中的宇宙可分割性概念，提出基于并查集的预处理策略将原问题分解为独立子问题，使用GRASP元启发式求解子问题并组合结果，显著提升了大规模可分解实例的求解质量和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小集合覆盖问题作为经典的NP难组合优化问题，现有方法大多将其视为整体处理，忽略了宇宙元素之间可能存在的内在结构特性。本文旨在探索宇宙可分割性这一概念，研究如何利用内在结构分解来增强启发式优化效果。

Method: 1. 提出基于并查集的高效预处理策略，通过元素在子集中的共现关系检测连通分量；2. 将原始实例分解为独立的子问题；3. 使用GRASP元启发式算法求解每个子问题；4. 在不影响可行性的前提下组合部分解；5. 采用简洁的位级集合表示实现高效集合操作。

Result: 在标准基准实例和大规模合成数据集上的广泛实验表明：利用自然宇宙分割能持续改进解的质量和可扩展性，特别是对于大规模且结构可分解的实例。所提出的方法在计算上具有实际可行性。

Conclusion: 宇宙可分割性是MSCP中一个有价值的结构特性，通过并查集预处理检测连通分量并分解问题，结合GRASP元启发式求解，能够显著提升大规模可分解实例的求解性能。简洁的位级集合表示确保了计算效率。

Abstract: The Minimum Set Cover Problem (MSCP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with numerous applications in science and engineering. Although a wide range of exact, approximate, and metaheuristic approaches have been proposed, most methods implicitly treat MSCP instances as monolithic, overlooking potential intrinsic structural properties of the universe. In this work, we investigate the concept of \emph{universe segmentability} in the MSCP and analyze how intrinsic structural decomposition (universe segmentability) can be exploited to enhance heuristic optimization. We propose an efficient preprocessing strategy based on disjoint-set union (union--find) to detect connected components induced by element co-occurrence within subsets, enabling the decomposition of the original instance into independent subproblems. Each subproblem is solved using the GRASP metaheuristic, and partial solutions are combined without compromising feasibility. Extensive experiments on standard benchmark instances and large-scale synthetic datasets show that exploiting natural universe segmentation consistently improves solution quality and scalability, particularly for large and structurally decomposable instances. These gains are supported by a succinct bit-level set representation that enables efficient set operations, making the proposed approach computationally practical at scale.

</details>


### [90] [Position: Science of AI Evaluation Requires Item-level Benchmark Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03244)
*Han Jiang,Susu Zhang,Xiaoyuan Yi,Xing Xie,Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该立场论文主张AI评估需要从基准层面转向项目层面分析，以解决当前评估范式的系统性效度问题，并介绍了OpenEval项目级基准数据仓库


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估范式存在系统性效度失败，包括不合理的设计选择和错位的评估指标，这些问题在没有系统化效度证据收集框架的情况下难以解决。AI评估已成为高风险领域部署生成式AI系统的主要依据，但现有方法缺乏严谨的科学基础。

Method: 通过分析计算机科学和心理测量学中的评估范式，提出项目级AI基准数据分析的必要性。通过项目属性分析和潜在构念检验，展示项目级数据提供的独特洞察。引入OpenEval——一个不断增长的项目级基准数据仓库，支持以证据为中心的AI评估。

Result: 项目级分析能够实现细粒度诊断和基准的严谨验证，为解决当前评估效度问题提供了方法论基础。OpenEval仓库的建立为社区采用项目级评估方法提供了实践支持。

Conclusion: 建立严谨的AI评估科学需要项目级基准数据，这种数据能够支持精细诊断和系统化效度验证。OpenEval的推出旨在推动社区向以证据为中心的AI评估范式转变。

Abstract: AI evaluations have become the primary evidence for deploying generative AI systems across high-stakes domains. However, current evaluation paradigms often exhibit systemic validity failures. These issues, ranging from unjustified design choices to misaligned metrics, remain intractable without a principled framework for gathering validity evidence and conducting granular diagnostic analysis. In this position paper, we argue that item-level AI benchmark data is essential for establishing a rigorous science of AI evaluation. Item-level analysis enables fine-grained diagnostics and principled validation of benchmarks. We substantiate this position by dissecting current validity failures and revisiting evaluation paradigms across computer science and psychometrics. Through illustrative analyses of item properties and latent constructs, we demonstrate the unique insights afforded by item-level data. To catalyze community-wide adoption, we introduce OpenEval, a growing repository of item-level benchmark data designed supporting evidence-centered AI evaluation.

</details>


### [91] [Evaluating Artificial Intelligence Through a Christian Understanding of Human Flourishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03356)
*Nicholas Skytland,Lauren Parsons,Alicia Llewellyn,Steele Billings,Peter Larson,John Anderson,Sean Boisen,Steve Runge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AI对齐不仅是安全问题，更是塑造问题，引入FAI-C-ST基准评估前沿模型在基督教人类繁荣七个维度上的表现，发现模型默认程序性世俗主义，导致系统性表现下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型越来越多地介入道德讨论和精神探索，它们不仅是信息提供者，更成为数字教理问答工具，积极塑造人类理解、决策和道德反思。需要使这种塑造影响变得可见和可测量，评估AI系统是否世界观中立

Method: 引入Flourishing AI Benchmark: Christian Single-Turn (FAI-C-ST)框架，设计用于评估前沿模型响应在基督教人类繁荣七个维度上的表现。比较20个前沿模型在多元主义和基督教特定标准下的表现

Result: 当前AI系统并非世界观中立，而是默认采用程序性世俗主义，缺乏维持神学一致性的基础，导致所有繁荣维度上系统性表现下降约17点。最关键的是，信仰和灵性维度下降31点

Conclusion: 价值观对齐中的表现差距不是技术限制，而是源于训练目标优先考虑广泛可接受性和安全性，而非深入、内部一致的道德或神学推理。AI对齐根本上是一个塑造问题，而不仅仅是安全问题

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) alignment is fundamentally a formation problem, not only a safety problem. As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly mediate moral deliberation and spiritual inquiry, they do more than provide information; they function as instruments of digital catechesis, actively shaping and ordering human understanding, decision-making, and moral reflection. To make this formative influence visible and measurable, we introduce the Flourishing AI Benchmark: Christian Single-Turn (FAI-C-ST), a framework designed to evaluate Frontier Model responses against a Christian understanding of human flourishing across seven dimensions.
  By comparing 20 Frontier Models against both pluralistic and Christian-specific criteria, we show that current AI systems are not worldview-neutral. Instead, they default to a Procedural Secularism that lacks the grounding necessary to sustain theological coherence, resulting in a systematic performance decline of approximately 17 points across all dimensions of flourishing. Most critically, there is a 31-point decline in the Faith and Spirituality dimension. These findings suggest that the performance gap in values alignment is not a technical limitation, but arises from training objectives that prioritize broad acceptability and safety over deep, internally coherent moral or theological reasoning.

</details>


### [92] [VERT: Reliable LLM Judges for Radiology Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03376)
*Federica Bologna,Jean-Philippe Corbeil,Matthew Wilkens,Asma Ben Abacha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了放射学报告评估中LLM作为评判者的性能，提出了新的评估指标VERT，并通过微调实现了显著的性能提升和推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前放射学报告评估研究主要集中于设计基于LLM的指标和微调小型模型用于胸部X光，但这些方法在其他模态和解剖部位的报告中是否稳健尚不明确。需要确定最适合作为放射学评估LLM评判者的模型和提示配置。

Method: 1) 在RadEval和RaTE-Eval两个专家标注数据集上进行专家评分与LLM评分的相关性分析；2) 比较三种现有LLM评判指标(RadFact、GREEN、FineRadScore)与提出的VERT指标；3) 使用不同规模的开源和闭源模型(推理型和非推理型)；4) 评估少样本方法、集成学习和参数高效微调；5) 进行系统错误检测和分类研究以评估指标与专家判断的一致性。

Result: 1) VERT相对于GREEN将放射科医生判断的相关性提高了11.7%；2) 仅使用1,300个训练样本微调Qwen3 30B模型可获得高达25%的性能提升；3) 微调模型将推理时间减少了高达37.2倍；4) 研究揭示了LLM评判指标与专家判断在不同错误类型上的一致性和差异。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的评判者在放射学报告评估中是有效的，通过轻量级适应可以实现可靠的评估。微调方法不仅显著提升性能，还能大幅降低推理时间，为实际临床应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Current literature on radiology report evaluation has focused primarily on designing LLM-based metrics and fine-tuning small models for chest X-rays. However, it remains unclear whether these approaches are robust when applied to reports from other modalities and anatomies. Which model and prompt configurations are best suited to serve as LLM judges for radiology evaluation? We conduct a thorough correlation analysis between expert and LLM-based ratings. We compare three existing LLM-as-a-judge metrics (RadFact, GREEN, and FineRadScore) alongside VERT, our proposed LLM-based metric, using open- and closed-source models (reasoning and non-reasoning) of different sizes across two expert-annotated datasets, RadEval and RaTE-Eval, spanning multiple modalities and anatomies. We further evaluate few-shot approaches, ensembling, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning using RaTE-Eval. To better understand metric behavior, we perform a systematic error detection and categorization study to assess alignment of these metrics against expert judgments and identify areas of lower and higher agreement. Our results show that VERT improves correlation with radiologist judgments by up to 11.7% relative to GREEN. Furthermore, fine-tuning Qwen3 30B yield gains of up to 25% using only 1,300 training samples. The fine-tuned model also reduces inference time up to 37.2 times. These findings highlight the effectiveness of LLM-based judges and demonstrate that reliable evaluation can be achieved with lightweight adaptation.

</details>


### [93] [Hume's Representational Conditions for Causal Judgment: What Bayesian Formalization Abstracted Away](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03387)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从休谟文本中提取了因果判断的三个表征条件，并追踪了从休谟到贝叶斯认识论和预测处理的演变，指出现代框架保留了休谟的更新结构但抽象掉了这些表征条件，以大型语言模型为例说明现代系统如何缺乏这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在阐明休谟因果判断理论中的三个核心表征条件，并分析这些条件在后续形式化框架中的演变命运，揭示现代计算系统（如大型语言模型）如何使这些原本隐含的条件变得可见。

Method: 1. 从休谟文本中提取三个表征条件：经验基础、结构化检索和生动性转移；2. 论证这些条件是休谟因果心理学的组成部分；3. 追踪从休谟到贝叶斯认识论和预测处理的形式化轨迹；4. 以大型语言模型为案例，展示现代系统如何体现统计更新但缺乏休谟的三个条件。

Result: 1. 成功识别并论证了休谟因果判断的三个表征条件；2. 展示了后续形式化框架（贝叶斯认识论、预测处理）保留了休谟的更新结构，但抽象掉了经验基础、结构化检索和生动性转移这些具体表征条件；3. 通过大型语言模型案例，揭示了现代计算系统如何使这些原本隐含的条件变得显性，因为它们进行统计更新但不满足休谟的三个条件。

Conclusion: 休谟的因果判断理论包含三个重要的表征条件，这些条件在后续的形式化框架中被抽象掉，但通过分析现代计算系统（如大型语言模型）可以发现，这些条件仍然是理解人类认知与机器认知差异的关键维度，使原本隐含的假设变得可见。

Abstract: Hume's account of causal judgment presupposes three representational conditions: experiential grounding (ideas must trace to impressions), structured retrieval (association must operate through organized networks exceeding pairwise connection), and vivacity transfer (inference must produce felt conviction, not merely updated probability). This paper extracts these conditions from Hume's texts and argues that they are integral to his causal psychology. It then traces their fate through the formalization trajectory from Hume to Bayesian epistemology and predictive processing, showing that later frameworks preserve the updating structure of Hume's insight while abstracting away these further representational conditions. Large language models serve as an illustrative contemporary case: they exhibit a form of statistical updating without satisfying the three conditions, thereby making visible requirements that were previously background assumptions in Hume's framework.

</details>


### [94] [TABQAWORLD: Optimizing Multimodal Reasoning for Multi-Turn Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03393)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Xinyu Wang,Xiaofeng Lin,Peng Lu,Chunhe Wang,Changlun Li,Hanwei Wu,Nan Tang,Elisa Kreiss,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TABQAWORLD是一个免训练的表格推理框架，通过联合优化表示和估计来提升多模态表格推理的可靠性和效率，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮表格推理方法依赖固定的文本序列化进行表格状态读取，导致表示误差在多轮中累积。表格接地方法虽然缓解了这一问题，但增加了推理计算和成本，使得实际部署不切实际。

Method: TABQAWORLD采用双管齐下的方法：1) 表示方面：使用动作条件化的多模态选择策略，动态切换视觉和文本表示以最大化表格状态读取可靠性；2) 估计方面：利用表格元数据（维度、数据类型、键值）优化逐步推理轨迹，安全规划轨迹并压缩低复杂度动作以减少对话轮次和延迟。

Result: 实验评估显示，TABQAWORLD实现了最先进的性能，相比基线准确率提升4.87%，相比静态设置获得5.42%的准确率提升和33.35%的推理延迟降低。

Conclusion: TABQAWORLD为可靠且高效的表格推理设立了新标准，通过联合优化表示和估计，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低计算成本，解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing reasoning capabilities of reasoning models. While multi-turn table reasoning methods have improved reasoning accuracy through tool use and reward modeling, they rely on fixed text serialization for table state readouts. This introduces representation errors in table encoding that significantly accumulate over multiple turns. Such accumulation is alleviated by tabular grounding methods in the expense of inference compute and cost, rendering real world deployment impractical. To address this, we introduce TABQAWORLD, a table reasoning framework that jointly optimizes tabular action through representation and estimation. For representation, TABQAWORLD employs an action-conditioned multimodal selection policy, which dynamically switches between visual and textual representations to maximize table state readout reliability. For estimation, TABQAWORLD optimizes stepwise reasoning trajectory through table metadata including dimension, data types and key values, safely planning trajectory and compressing low-complexity actions to reduce conversation turns and latency. Designed as a training-free framework, empirical evaluations show that TABQAWORLD achieves state-of-the-art performance with 4.87% accuracy improvements over baselines, with 5.42% accuracy gain and 33.35% inference latency reduction over static settings, establishing a new standard for reliable and efficient table reasoning.

</details>


### [95] [Contextual Control without Memory Growth in a Context-Switching Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03479)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种通过干预共享循环潜在状态来实现上下文依赖的第三种方法，无需扩大循环维度或直接提供上下文输入


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决上下文依赖的顺序决策问题，传统方法要么直接提供上下文作为输入，要么增加循环记忆来内部表示上下文信息。本文研究第三种替代方案：通过对共享循环潜在状态进行干预来实现上下文依赖，而不扩大循环维度。

Method: 引入基于干预的循环架构：循环核心首先构建共享的预干预潜在状态，然后上下文通过加性的、上下文索引的算子进行干预。在部分可观测的上下文切换顺序决策任务上评估该方法，并与标签辅助基线（直接上下文访问）和记忆基线（扩大循环状态）进行比较。

Result: 在主要基准测试中，干预模型在不增加循环维度的情况下表现强劲。使用条件互信息(I(C;O|S))作为理论驱动的操作探针评估上下文依赖，对于任务相关的阶段1结果，干预模型显示出正的条件上下文信息。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在共享循环状态上进行干预为上下文控制提供了一种可行的替代方案，无需扩大循环记忆，在该设置下具有良好性能。

Abstract: Context-dependent sequential decision making is commonly addressed either by providing context explicitly as an input or by increasing recurrent memory so that contextual information can be represented internally. We study a third alternative: realizing contextual dependence by intervening on a shared recurrent latent state, without enlarging recurrent dimensionality. To this end, we introduce an intervention-based recurrent architecture in which a recurrent core first constructs a shared pre-intervention latent state, and context then acts through an additive, context-indexed operator. We evaluate this idea on a context-switching sequential decision task under partial observability. We compare three model families: a label-assisted baseline with direct context access, a memory baseline with enlarged recurrent state, and the proposed intervention model, which uses no direct context input to the recurrent core and no memory growth. On the main benchmark, the intervention model performs strongly without additional recurrent dimensions. We also evaluate the models using the conditional mutual information (I(C;O | S)) as a theorem-motivated operational probe of contextual dependence at fixed latent state. For task-relevant phase-1 outcomes, the intervention model exhibits positive conditional contextual information. Together, these results suggest that intervention on a shared recurrent state provides a viable alternative to recurrent memory growth for contextual control in this setting.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Predefined Schemas: TRACE-KG for Context-Enriched Knowledge Graphs from Complex Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03496)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Yang Ba,Yixuan He,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-KG是一个多模态框架，无需预定义本体即可联合构建上下文丰富的知识图谱和推导出的模式，提供结构化限定符和可重用语义支架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱构建方法存在局限性：基于本体的方法需要昂贵的模式设计和维护成本，而无模式方法通常产生碎片化的图谱，缺乏全局组织，特别是在包含密集、上下文依赖信息的长技术文档中。

Method: TRACE-KG是一个多模态框架，通过结构化限定符捕获条件关系，并使用数据驱动模式组织实体和关系，该模式作为可重用的语义支架，同时保持对源证据的完全可追溯性。

Result: 实验表明，TRACE-KG能够生成结构连贯、可追溯的知识图谱，为基于本体和无模式的构建流程提供了实用的替代方案。

Conclusion: TRACE-KG框架在无需预定义本体的前提下，能够构建上下文丰富的知识图谱并推导出可重用的模式，有效解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph construction typically relies either on predefined ontologies or on schema-free extraction. Ontology-driven pipelines enforce consistent typing but require costly schema design and maintenance, whereas schema-free methods often produce fragmented graphs with weak global organization, especially in long technical documents with dense, context-dependent information. We propose TRACE-KG (Text-dRiven schemA for Context-Enriched Knowledge Graphs), a multimodal framework that jointly constructs a context-enriched knowledge graph and an induced schema without assuming a predefined ontology. TRACE-KG captures conditional relations through structured qualifiers and organizes entities and relations using a data-driven schema that serves as a reusable semantic scaffold while preserving full traceability to the source evidence. Experiments show that TRACE-KG produces structurally coherent, traceable knowledge graphs and offers a practical alternative to both ontology-driven and schema-free construction pipelines.

</details>


### [97] [Resource-Conscious Modeling for Next- Day Discharge Prediction Using Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03498)
*Ha Na Cho,Sairam Sutari,Alexander Lopez,Hansen Bow,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了轻量级微调大语言模型与传统文本模型在脊柱择期手术中预测次日出院的效果，发现TF-IDF结合LGBM模型在性能与效率上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱择期手术中及时预测出院对于优化床位周转和资源配置至关重要，需要探索高效且可解释的预测模型。

Method: 比较了13种模型，包括TF-IDF结合XGBoost和LGBM的传统模型，以及通过LoRA微调的轻量级大语言模型（DistilGPT-2、Bio_ClinicalBERT）。

Result: TF-IDF结合LGBM模型表现最佳：出院类F1分数0.47，召回率0.51，AUC-ROC最高（0.80）。LoRA提升了DistilGPT2的召回率，但整体上基于Transformer和生成模型表现不佳。

Conclusion: 在现实世界不平衡临床预测任务中，可解释且资源高效的模型可能优于轻量级大语言模型。

Abstract: Timely discharge prediction is essential for optimizing bed turnover and resource allocation in elective spine surgery units. This study evaluates the feasibility of lightweight, fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) and traditional text-based models for predicting next-day discharge using postoperative clinical notes. We compared 13 models, including TF-IDF with XGBoost and LGBM, and compact LLMs (DistilGPT-2, Bio_ClinicalBERT) fine-tuned via LoRA. TF-IDF with LGBM achieved the best balance, with an F1-score of 0.47 for the discharge class, a recall of 0.51, and the highest AUC-ROC (0.80). While LoRA improved recall in DistilGPT2, overall transformer-based and generative models underperformed. These findings suggest interpretable, resource-efficient models may outperform compact LLMs in real-world, imbalanced clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [98] [BioAlchemy: Distilling Biological Literature into Reasoning-Ready Reinforcement Learning Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03506)
*Brian Hsu,Ozan Gökdemir,Carlo Siebenschuh,Bruce Parrello,Neil Getty,Thomas S. Brettin,Rick L. Stevens,Ian T. Foster,Nicholas Chia,Arvind Ramanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioAlchemy：从生物学研究文本中提取多样化可验证问答对的流程，创建了34.5万个生物学科学推理问题的训练数据集，并通过强化学习提升生物学推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模推理数据集的生物学问题与现代生物学研究主题分布不匹配，这种主题不平衡可能影响性能；从生物学研究文本中提取具有挑战性且可验证的研究问题的方法在应用强化学习提升生物学研究任务性能方面至关重要但尚未充分发展

Method: 引入BioAlchemy流程，从生物学研究文本的科学语料库中获取多样化的可验证问答对；创建BioAlchemy-345K训练数据集；通过强化学习使数据集与当代科学生物学主题分布对齐以提升推理性能

Result: 开发了BioAlchemist-8B模型，相比其基础推理模型在生物学基准测试上提升了9.12%；数据集包含超过34.5万个生物学科学推理问题

Conclusion: 该方法能有效增强生物学领域的科学推理能力；BioAlchemist-8B模型已在Hugging Face平台发布

Abstract: Despite the large corpus of biology training text, the impact of reasoning models on biological research generally lags behind math and coding. In this work, we show that biology questions from current large-scale reasoning datasets do not align well with modern research topic distributions in biology, and that this topic imbalance may negatively affect performance. In addition, we find that methods for extracting challenging and verifiable research problems from biology research text are a critical yet underdeveloped ingredient in applying reinforcement learning for better performance on biology research tasks. We introduce BioAlchemy, a pipeline for sourcing a diverse set of verifiable question-and-answer pairs from a scientific corpus of biology research text. We curate BioAlchemy-345K, a training dataset containing over 345K scientific reasoning problems in biology. Then, we demonstrate how aligning our dataset to the topic distribution of modern scientific biology can be used with reinforcement learning to improve reasoning performance. Finally, we present BioAlchemist-8B, which improves over its base reasoning model by 9.12% on biology benchmarks. These results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach for developing stronger scientific reasoning capabilities in biology. The BioAlchemist-8B model is available at: https://huggingface.co/BioAlchemy.

</details>


### [99] [Structural Rigidity and the 57-Token Predictive Window: A Physical Framework for Inference-Layer Governability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03524)
*Gregory M. Ruddell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于能量的治理框架，通过轨迹张力分析发现预承诺信号仅存在于特定模型配置中，建立了推理行为的五类分类法，证明规则违反和幻觉是不同的故障模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全依赖行为监控和训练后对齐，但实证测量显示这些方法在多数指令调优模型中无法检测到预承诺信号。需要建立可测量的推理层治理框架来评估自主AI系统的部署风险

Method: 提出基于能量的治理框架，将Transformer推理动态连接到神经计算的约束满足模型。使用轨迹张力(rho = ||a|| / ||v||)作为分析指标，在七模型队列中应用五几何机制分类法，通过能量不对称性作为结构刚度的统一度量

Result: 在Phi-3-mini-4k-instruct模型中发现57个token的预承诺窗口，但该信号是模型、任务和配置特定的。建立了五类推理行为分类：权威带、晚期信号、倒置、平坦和支架选择性。七个模型中只有一个配置显示预测性信号，其他显示静默失败、晚期检测、倒置动态或平坦几何。事实幻觉在72个测试条件下均无预测信号

Conclusion: 规则违反和幻觉是不同的故障模式，需要不同的检测方法。内部几何监控仅在存在阻力时有效，事实虚构的检测需要外部验证机制。该工作为推理层可治理性提供了可测量框架，并为评估自主AI系统的部署风险引入了分类法

Abstract: Current AI safety relies on behavioral monitoring and post-training alignment, yet empirical measurement shows these approaches produce no detectable pre-commitment signal in a majority of instruction-tuned models tested. We present an energy-based governance framework connecting transformer inference dynamics to constraint-satisfaction models of neural computation, and apply it to a seven-model cohort across five geometric regimes.
  Using trajectory tension (rho = ||a|| / ||v||), we identify a 57-token pre-commitment window in Phi-3-mini-4k-instruct under greedy decoding on arithmetic constraint probes. This result is model-specific, task-specific, and configuration-specific, demonstrating that pre-commitment signals can exist but are not universal.
  We introduce a five-regime taxonomy of inference behavior: Authority Band, Late Signal, Inverted, Flat, and Scaffold-Selective. Energy asymmetry (Σ\r{ho}_misaligned / Σ\r{ho}_aligned) serves as a unifying metric of structural rigidity across these regimes.
  Across seven models, only one configuration exhibits a predictive signal prior to commitment; all others show silent failure, late detection, inverted dynamics, or flat geometry.
  We further demonstrate that factual hallucination produces no predictive signal across 72 test conditions, consistent with spurious attractor settling in the absence of a trained world-model constraint.
  These results establish that rule violation and hallucination are distinct failure modes with different detection requirements. Internal geometry monitoring is effective only where resistance exists; detection of factual confabulation requires external verification mechanisms.
  This work provides a measurable framework for inference-layer governability and introduces a taxonomy for evaluating deployment risk in autonomous AI systems.

</details>


### [100] [Explainable Model Routing for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03527)
*Mika Okamoto,Ansel Kaplan Erol,Mark Riedl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Topaz是一个为智能体路由系统引入可审计性的框架，通过技能分析、可追溯路由算法和开发者解释，使模型选择决策变得可解释和可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体工作流中的路由架构只关注性能优化，没有记录模型能力与成本之间的权衡关系。开发者无法区分智能效率（为合适任务选择专门模型）和预算驱动模型选择导致的潜在失败，需要可审计的路由决策。

Method: Topaz包含三个核心组件：1）基于技能的分析，将多样化基准测试的性能合成为细粒度能力档案；2）完全可追溯的路由算法，使用基于预算和多目标优化，清晰记录技能匹配分数与成本的权衡过程；3）面向开发者的解释，将这些追踪信息转换为自然语言，支持系统逻辑审计和成本-质量权衡的迭代调整。

Result: Topaz通过使路由决策可解释，让用户能够理解、信任并有意义地引导路由智能体系统，解决了当前路由架构缺乏透明度和可审计性的问题。

Conclusion: Topaz框架为智能体路由系统引入了形式化的可审计性，通过透明化的路由决策过程，使开发者能够审计系统逻辑并迭代调整成本-质量权衡，从而增强用户对路由智能体系统的理解和信任。

Abstract: Modern agentic workflows decompose complex tasks into specialized subtasks and route them to diverse models to minimize cost without sacrificing quality. However, current routing architectures focus exclusively on performance optimization, leaving underlying trade-offs between model capability and cost unrecorded. Without clear rationale, developers cannot distinguish between intelligent efficiency -- using specialized models for appropriate tasks -- and latent failures caused by budget-driven model selection. We present Topaz, a framework that introduces formal auditability to agentic routing. Topaz replaces silent model assignments with an inherently interpretable router that incorporates three components: (i) skill-based profiling that synthesizes performance across diverse benchmarks into granular capability profiles (ii) fully traceable routing algorithms that utilize budget-based and multi-objective optimization to produce clear traces of how skill-match scores were weighed against costs, and (iii) developer-facing explanations that translate these traces into natural language, allowing users to audit system logic and iteratively tune the cost-quality tradeoff. By making routing decisions interpretable, Topaz enables users to understand, trust, and meaningfully steer routed agentic systems.

</details>


### [101] [Automated Analysis of Global AI Safety Initiatives: A Taxonomy-Driven LLM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03533)
*Takayuki Semitsu,Naoto Kiribuchi,Kengo Zenitani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自动交叉比对框架，使用AI安全活动分类法比较政策文档对，评估LLM方法的稳定性与有效性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统化比较AI安全政策文档，评估基于大语言模型的交叉比对分析的稳定性和有效性

Method: 基于AI安全活动地图分类法，构建自动交叉比对框架；使用5个LLM对10个公开文档进行交叉比对，通过热图可视化平均相似度得分；进行3位专家的人工评估验证

Result: 模型选择显著影响交叉比对结果，部分文档对在不同模型间存在高度分歧；人工评估显示高标注者间一致性，但模型得分与人工判断仍存在差异

Conclusion: 支持政策文档的比较性检查，但需注意模型选择对结果的影响，模型与人类判断存在差异

Abstract: We present an automated crosswalk framework that compares an AI safety policy document pair under a shared taxonomy of activities. Using the activity categories defined in Activity Map on AI Safety as fixed aspects, the system extracts and maps relevant activities, then produces for each aspect a short summary for each document, a brief comparison, and a similarity score. We assess the stability and validity of LLM-based crosswalk analysis across public policy documents. Using five large language models, we perform crosswalks on ten publicly available documents and visualize mean similarity scores with a heatmap. The results show that model choice substantially affects the crosswalk outcomes, and that some document pairs yield high disagreements across models. A human evaluation by three experts on two document pairs shows high inter-annotator agreement, while model scores still differ from human judgments. These findings support comparative inspection of policy documents.

</details>


### [102] [When Do Hallucinations Arise? A Graph Perspective on the Evolution of Path Reuse and Path Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03557)
*Xinnan Dai,Kai Yang,Cheng Luo,Shenglai Zeng,Kai Guo,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM的推理幻觉建模为图搜索过程，揭示了路径重用和路径压缩两种机制导致幻觉产生


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM中的推理幻觉（流畅但无依据的结论）被广泛观察到，但解码器Transformer产生这些幻觉的机制仍然不清楚，需要从计算角度理解其根源

Method: 将下一个token预测建模为图搜索过程：实体对应节点，学习到的转换形成边。上下文推理是在采样子图上的约束搜索（内在推理），而无上下文查询则依赖底层图中的记忆结构（外在推理）

Result: 揭示了推理幻觉的两种基本机制：1) 路径重用：早期训练中记忆的知识覆盖上下文约束；2) 路径压缩：后期训练中频繁遍历的多步路径坍缩为捷径边。这些机制为LLM中的推理幻觉提供了统一解释

Conclusion: 路径重用和路径压缩机制共同解释了LLM中的推理幻觉现象，并与下游应用中观察到的已知行为相关联，为理解Transformer的推理失败提供了理论基础

Abstract: Reasoning hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) often appear as fluent yet unsupported conclusions that violate either the given context or underlying factual knowledge. Although such failures are widely observed, the mechanisms by which decoder-only Transformers produce them remain poorly understood. We model next-token prediction as a graph search process over an underlying graph, where entities correspond to nodes and learned transitions form edges. From this perspective, contextual reasoning is a constrained search over a sampled subgraph (intrinsic reasoning), while context-free queries rely on memorized structures in the underlying graph (extrinsic reasoning). We show that reasoning hallucinations arise from two fundamental mechanisms: \textbf{Path Reuse}, where memorized knowledge overrides contextual constraints during early training, and \textbf{Path Compression}, where frequently traversed multi-step paths collapse into shortcut edges in later training. Together, these mechanisms provide a unified explanation for reasoning hallucinations in LLMs and connected to well-known behaviors observed in downstream applications.

</details>


### [103] [When Adaptive Rewards Hurt: Causal Probing and the Switching-Stability Dilemma in LLM-Guided LEO Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03562)
*Yuanhang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现自适应奖励权重在深度强化学习中存在切换-稳定性困境：恒定权重优于动态权重，因为PPO需要准平稳奖励信号；提出因果探测方法揭示奖励项的非直观影响；评估四种MDP架构变体，发现MLP表现最佳，微调LLM因权重振荡而崩溃


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于直觉：在多波束LEO卫星调度中，基于状态感知的自适应奖励权重应优于静态权重。研究者希望系统性地验证这一直觉，并探索奖励权重设计对深度强化学习性能的影响机制。

Method: 1) 系统测试自适应奖励权重与静态权重的性能对比；2) 提出单变量因果探测方法，独立扰动每个奖励项±20%，测量PPO在5万步后的响应；3) 评估四种MDP架构变体：固定权重、基于规则的权重、学习的MLP权重、微调的LLM权重；4) 在已知和新型流量状态下进行测试。

Result: 1) 恒定权重(342.1 Mbps)优于动态权重(103.3±96.8 Mbps)，揭示切换-稳定性困境；2) 因果探测发现非直观杠杆效应：切换惩罚增加20%带来极地切换+157 Mbps、热冷状态+130 Mbps增益；3) MLP在已知状态达到357.9 Mbps，新型状态325.2 Mbps；4) 微调LLM崩溃至45.3±43.0 Mbps，因权重振荡而非领域知识不足。

Conclusion: PPO需要准平稳奖励信号进行价值函数收敛，权重自适应会重启收敛过程而损害性能。LLM-DRL集成的关键约束是输出一致性而非领域知识。研究为通信系统中LLM-DRL集成提供了经验路线图，明确了LLM的不可替代价值（自然语言意图理解）与简单方法适用的场景。

Abstract: Adaptive reward design for deep reinforcement learning (DRL) in multi-beam LEO satellite scheduling is motivated by the intuition that regime-aware reward weights should outperform static ones. We systematically test this intuition and uncover a switching-stability dilemma: near-constant reward weights (342.1 Mbps) outperform carefully-tuned dynamic weights (103.3+/-96.8 Mbps) because PPO requires a quasistationary reward signal for value function convergence. Weight adaptation-regardless of quality-degrades performance by repeatedly restarting convergence. To understand why specific weights matter, we introduce a single-variable causal probing method that independently perturbs each reward term by +/-20% and measures PPO response after 50k steps. Probing reveals counterintuitive leverage: a +20% increase in the switching penalty yields +157 Mbps for polar handover and +130 Mbps for hot-cold regimes-findings inaccessible to human experts or trained MLPs without systematic probing. We evaluate four MDP architect variants (fixed, rule-based, learned MLP, finetuned LLM) across known and novel traffic regimes. The MLP achieves 357.9 Mbps on known regimes and 325.2 Mbps on novel regimes, while the fine-tuned LLM collapses to 45.3+/-43.0 Mbps due to weight oscillation rather than lack of domain knowledge-output consistency, not knowledge, is the binding constraint. Our findings provide an empirically-grounded roadmap for LLM-DRL integration in communication systems, identifying where LLMs add irreplaceable value (natural language intent understanding) versus where simpler methods suffice.

</details>


### [104] [Personality Requires Struggle: Three Regimes of the Baldwin Effect in Neuroevolved Chess Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03565)
*Diego Armando Resendez Prado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究发现Hebbian可塑性在进化过程中对行为多样性的影响存在反转：初期压缩多样性，后期通过想象放大感知差异，形成结构化行为分化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究终身学习是否会随时间推移扩展行为多样性而非压缩多样性，挑战传统理论认为可塑性通过缓冲环境噪声减少变异的观点

Method: 使用具有8个NEAT进化神经模块的象棋智能体，包含Hebbian游戏内可塑性、期望域信号链和想象机制，比较10个种子的Hebbian开启/关闭条件

Result: 发现方差交叉现象：Hebbian开启初期跨种子方差低于关闭条件，但在第34代后反超；形成结构化行为分化（62%走棋分歧、不同开局库、棋子偏好、游戏时长）；识别三种机制：探索、彩票和透明机制

Conclusion: 可塑性对行为多样性的影响在进化时间尺度上发生反转，通过想象放大感知差异形成反馈循环；自我对弈系统可能系统性抑制行为多样性，消除个性所需的异质性

Abstract: Can lifetime learning expand behavioral diversity over evolutionary time, rather than collapsing it? Prior theory predicts that plasticity reduces variance by buffering organisms against environmental noise. We test this in a competitive domain: chess agents with eight NEAT-evolved neural modules, Hebbian within-game plasticity, and a desirability-domain signal chain with imagination. Across 10~seeds per Hebbian condition, a variance crossover emerges: Hebbian ON starts with lower cross-seed variance than OFF, then surpasses it at generation~34. The crossover trend is monotonic (\r{ho} = 0.91, p < 10^{-6): plasticity's effect on behavioral variance reverses over evolutionary time, initially compressing diversity (consistent with prior predictions) then expanding it as evolved Perception differences are amplified through imagination -- a feedback loop that mutation alone cannot sustain.
  The result is structured behavioral divergence: evolved agents select different moves on the same positions (62\% disagreement), develop distinct opening repertoires, piece preferences, and game lengths. These are not different sampling policies -- they are reproducible behavioral signatures (ICC > 0.8) with interpretable signal chain configurations. Three regimes appear depending on opponent type: exploration (Hebbian ON, heterogeneous opponent), lottery (Hebbian OFF, elitism lock-in), and transparent (same-model opponent, brain self-erasure). The transparent regime generates a falsifiable prediction: self-play systems may systematically suppress behavioral diversity by eliminating the heterogeneity that personality requires.
  \textbf{Keywords: Baldwin Effect, neuroevolution, NEAT, Hebbian learning, chess, cognitive architecture, personality emergence, imagination

</details>


### [105] [Selective Forgetting for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03571)
*Tuan Le,Wei Qian,Mengdi Huai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出针对大型推理模型的知识遗忘框架，通过选择性移除敏感推理组件来保护推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过结构化思维链生成答案，容易在中间推理步骤中泄露敏感信息（如版权和隐私内容），现有遗忘方法主要针对最终答案，可能损害模型的整体推理能力

Method: 提出选择性遗忘框架，利用多个LLM和检索增强生成分析思维链轨迹，识别需要遗忘的相关片段，用良性占位符替换，同时引入特征替换遗忘损失函数

Result: 在合成和医疗数据集上的广泛实验验证了所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该框架能够精确遗忘目标知识，同时保持一般推理能力的完整性，解决了LRM遗忘的关键挑战

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) generate structured chains of thought (CoTs) before producing final answers, making them especially vulnerable to knowledge leakage through intermediate reasoning steps. Yet, the memorization of sensitive information in the training data such as copyrighted and private content has led to ethical and legal concerns. To address these issues, selective forgetting (also known as machine unlearning) has emerged as a potential remedy for LRMs. However, existing unlearning methods primarily target final answers and may degrade the overall reasoning ability of LRMs after forgetting. Additionally, directly applying unlearning on the entire CoTs could degrade the general reasoning capabilities. The key challenge for LRM unlearning lies in achieving precise unlearning of targeted knowledge while preserving the integrity of general reasoning capabilities. To bridge this gap, we in this paper propose a novel LRM unlearning framework that selectively removes sensitive reasoning components while preserving general reasoning capabilities. Our approach leverages multiple LLMs with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to analyze CoT traces, identify forget-relevant segments, and replace them with benign placeholders that maintain logical structure. We also introduce a new feature replacement unlearning loss for LRMs, which can simultaneously suppress the probability of generating forgotten content while reinforcing structurally valid replacements. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and medical datasets verify the desired properties of our proposed method.

</details>


### [106] [Entropy and Attention Dynamics in Small Language Models: A Trace-Level Structural Analysis on the TruthfulQA Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03589)
*Adeyemi Adeseye,Aisvarya Adeseye,Hannu Tenhunen,Jouni Isoaho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究对小型语言模型在TruthfulQA数据集上的熵和注意力动态进行追踪分析，发现三种不同的模型分类：确定性模型、探索性模型和平衡模型，每种模型具有不同的内部不确定性模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型在资源受限环境中部署时存在自信的错误预测和不稳定输出问题，当前评估方法仅关注最终准确率或幻觉率，缺乏对内部模型行为如何影响输出的解释，特别是熵在解码过程中的演变、注意力在层间的分布以及隐藏表示如何影响不确定性、逻辑不一致和错误信息传播。

Method: 研究引入对小型语言模型的熵和注意力动态的追踪分析，使用TruthfulQA数据集评估四个参数范围在1B-1.7B的模型，通过令牌级输出熵、注意力熵、头部分散度和隐藏状态表示进行分析。

Result: 发现三种模型分类：确定性模型（DeepSeek-1.5B和LLaMA-1B）输出熵随时间减少；探索性模型（Gemma-1B）熵增加；平衡模型（Qwen-1.7B）具有适度且稳定的熵。每个组别具有明显不同的隐藏状态移动和注意力分散模式。

Conclusion: 小型语言模型的真实性源于结构化的熵和注意力动态，监测和优化这些内部不确定性模式可以指导设计更可靠、幻觉感知和特定应用的小型语言模型。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) have been increasingly deployed in edge devices and other resource-constrained settings. However, these models make confident mispredictions and produce unstable output, making them risky for factual and decision-critical tasks. Current evaluation methodology relies on final accuracy or hallucination rates without explaining how internal model behavior affects outputs. Specifically, how entropy evolves during decoding, how attention is distributed across layers, and how hidden representations contribute to uncertainty, logical inconsistencies, and misinformation propagation are often overlooked. Consequently, this study introduces a trace-level analysis of entropy and attention dynamics in SLMs evaluated with the TruthfulQA dataset. Four models with parameter ranges of 1B-1.7B parameters were examined via token-level output entropy, attention entropy, head dispersion, and hidden-state representation. The results reflect three model classifications by entropy patterns. Deterministic models (DeepSeek-1.5B and LLaMA-1B): output entropy decreases over time. Exploratory models (Gemma-1B): with increasing entropy, and balanced models (Qwen-1.7B): have moderate and stable entropy. Also, each group has distinctively different hidden-state movement and attention dispersion patterns. The analysis demonstrates that truthfulness in SLMs emerges from structured entropy and attention dynamics. Monitoring and optimizing these internal uncertainty patterns can guide the design of a more reliable, hallucination-aware, and application-specific edge SLMs.

</details>


### [107] [A Multimodal Foundation Model of Spatial Transcriptomics and Histology for Biological Discovery and Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03630)
*Jinxi Xiang,Siyu Hou,Yuchen Li,Ryan Quinton,Xiaoming Zhang,Feyisope Eweje,Xiangde Luo,Yijiang Chen,Zhe Li,Colin Bergstrom,Ted Kim,Sierra Willens,Francesca Maria Olguin,Matthew Abikenari,Andrew Heider,Sanjeeth Rajaram,Joel Neal,Maximilian Diehn,Xiang Zhou,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STORM是一个基础模型，通过整合空间转录组学和组织学数据，建立分子-形态学关联，用于从H&E图像预测基因表达并改善临床预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学(ST)成本高、通量低，而H&E染色虽提供丰富形态学信息但缺乏分子分辨率。需要一种能够桥接成像和组学数据的方法，实现从常规组织学图像中获取分子信息

Method: 开发STORM基础模型，在18个器官的120万个空间分辨转录组谱与匹配组织学数据上训练。采用分层架构整合形态特征、基因表达和空间上下文，建立稳健的分子-形态学表示

Result: STORM在11种肿瘤类型中从H&E图像预测空间基因表达方面优于现有方法，增强空间域发现并产生生物学一致的组织图谱。模型平台无关，在Visium、Xenium、Visium HD和CosMx上表现一致。应用于7245名患者的23个独立队列，显著改善免疫治疗反应预测和预后评估

Conclusion: STORM提供了一个可扩展的框架，用于空间信息发现和临床精准医学，通过整合组织学和转录组学数据实现从常规病理图像中提取分子信息

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables gene expression mapping within anatomical context but remains costly and low-throughput. Hematoxylin and eosin (H\&E) staining offers rich morphology yet lacks molecular resolution. We present \textbf{\ours} (\textbf{S}patial \textbf{T}ranscriptomics and hist\textbf{O}logy \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{M}odel), a foundation model trained on 1.2 million spatially resolved transcriptomic profiles with matched histology across 18 organs. Using a hierarchical architecture integrating morphological features, gene expression, and spatial context, STORM bridges imaging and omics through robust molecular--morphological representations. STORM enhances spatial domain discovery, producing biologically coherent tissue maps, and outperforms existing methods in predicting spatial gene expression from H\&E images across 11 tumor types. The model is platform-agnostic, performing consistently across Visium, Xenium, Visium HD, and CosMx. Applied to 23 independent cohorts comprising 7,245 patients, STORM significantly improves immunotherapy response prediction and prognostication over established biomarkers, providing a scalable framework for spatially informed discovery and clinical precision medicine.

</details>


### [108] [Single-agent vs. Multi-agents for Automated Video Analysis of On-Screen Collaborative Learning Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03631)
*Likai Peng,Shihui Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了闭源和开源视觉语言模型在单智能体与多智能体设置下，基于ICAP框架对协作学习场景中的屏幕录制视频进行自动编码的性能，提出了两种多智能体框架并验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕学习行为分析对于理解学生在学习过程中的信息寻求、使用和创造至关重要，但传统的手动编码方法劳动密集。视觉语言模型的发展为自动化多模态视频数据分析提供了新机遇，本研究旨在探索VLM在协作学习场景中自动编码屏幕行为的性能。

Method: 提出了两种多智能体框架：1）基于工作流的三智能体系统，通过场景分割、光标信息增强的VLM提示和基于证据的验证来检测屏幕行为；2）受ReAct启发的自主决策多智能体系统，迭代地进行推理、工具操作（分割/分类/验证）和观察驱动的自我修正。比较了闭源VLM（Claude-3.7-Sonnet, GPT-4.1）和开源VLM（Qwen2.5-VL-72B）在单智能体和多智能体设置下的性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，两种提出的多智能体框架均实现了可行性能，在场景和行为检测任务上优于单VLM。基于工作流的智能体在场景检测上表现最佳，而自主决策多智能体在行为检测上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了基于VLM的多智能体系统在视频分析中的有效性，为多模态数据分析提供了一个可扩展的框架，能够自动化协作学习场景中的屏幕行为编码任务。

Abstract: On-screen learning behavior provides valuable insights into how students seek, use, and create information during learning. Analyzing on-screen behavioral engagement is essential for capturing students' cognitive and collaborative processes. The recent development of Vision Language Models (VLMs) offers new opportunities to automate the labor-intensive manual coding often required for multimodal video data analysis. In this study, we compared the performance of both leading closed-source VLMs (Claude-3.7-Sonnet, GPT-4.1) and open-source VLM (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) in single- and multi-agent settings for automated coding of screen recordings in collaborative learning contexts based on the ICAP framework. In particular, we proposed and compared two multi-agent frameworks: 1) a three-agent workflow multi-agent system (MAS) that segments screen videos by scene and detects on-screen behaviors using cursor-informed VLM prompting with evidence-based verification; 2) an autonomous-decision MAS inspired by ReAct that iteratively interleaves reasoning, tool-like operations (segmentation/ classification/ validation), and observation-driven self-correction to produce interpretable on-screen behavior labels. Experimental results demonstrated that the two proposed MAS frameworks achieved viable performance, outperforming the single VLMs in scene and action detection tasks. It is worth noting that the workflow-based agent achieved best on scene detection, and the autonomous-decision MAS achieved best on action detection. This study demonstrates the effectiveness of VLM-based Multi-agent System for video analysis and contributes a scalable framework for multimodal data analytics.

</details>


### [109] [TableVision: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Spatially Grounded Reasoning over Complex Hierarchical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03660)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Lu Dai,Hanqing Wang,Zhuoyu Li,Wenbin Dai,Yanzong Zheng,Zhenggang Xia,Junyong Lin,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TableVision：针对结构化表格理解的大规模轨迹感知基准，通过显式空间约束解决MLLMs在复杂分层表格中的感知瓶颈问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在表格理解方面取得进展，但在处理具有分层布局的复杂表格时推理性能仍然有限。研究发现存在关键的感知瓶颈：随着任务复杂度增加，涉及的离散视觉区域数量不成比例增长，导致"感知过载"，使MLLMs在隐式生成过程中难以维持准确的空间注意力。

Method: 引入TableVision基准，包含三个认知层次（感知、推理、分析）的13个子类别。采用基于渲染的确定性定位管道，将多步逻辑推导与像素级空间真值显式耦合，构建了6,799个高保真推理轨迹。提出两阶段解耦框架，通过显式空间约束恢复MLLMs的推理潜力。

Result: 诊断性探测支持的实证结果表明，显式空间约束显著恢复了MLLMs的推理潜力。两阶段解耦框架在测试集上实现了12.3%的整体准确率提升。TableVision为文档理解中感知与逻辑的协同提供了严格测试平台和新视角。

Conclusion: TableVision通过显式空间约束解决了MLLMs在复杂表格理解中的感知瓶颈问题，为文档理解中感知与逻辑的协同研究提供了重要基准和方法框架。

Abstract: Structured tables are essential for conveying high-density information in professional domains such as finance, healthcare, and scientific research. Despite the progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), reasoning performance remains limited for complex tables with hierarchical layouts. In this paper, we identify a critical Perception Bottleneck through quantitative analysis. We find that as task complexity scales, the number of involved discrete visual regions increases disproportionately. This processing density leads to an internal "Perceptual Overload," where MLLMs struggle to maintain accurate spatial attention during implicit generation. To address this bottleneck, we introduce TableVision, a large-scale, trajectory-aware benchmark designed for spatially grounded reasoning. TableVision stratifies tabular tasks into three cognitive levels (Perception, Reasoning, and Analysis) across 13 sub-categories. By utilizing a rendering-based deterministic grounding pipeline, the dataset explicitly couples multi-step logical deductions with pixel-perfect spatial ground truths, comprising 6,799 high-fidelity reasoning trajectories. Our empirical results, supported by diagnostic probing, demonstrate that explicit spatial constraints significantly recover the reasoning potential of MLLMs. Furthermore, our two-stage decoupled framework achieves a robust 12.3% overall accuracy improvement on the test set. TableVision provides a rigorous testbed and a fresh perspective on the synergy between perception and logic in document understanding.

</details>


### [110] [PRAISE: Prefix-Based Rollout Reuse in Agentic Search Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03675)
*Erhan Zhang,Yiqun Chen,Zechun Niu,Wei Yang,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRAISE框架通过重用搜索轨迹前缀和推导中间奖励，解决了智能搜索中数据效率低和奖励稀疏的问题，在无需额外标注的情况下提升了多跳问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于搜索的强化学习方法存在两个核心限制：1）昂贵的多步搜索轨迹在训练中未充分利用；2）监督信号通常只在最终答案处可用，导致严重的奖励稀疏问题。这些问题影响了智能搜索（如多跳问答）的训练效率和效果。

Method: PRAISE框架从完整的搜索轨迹中提取不同搜索轮次的前缀状态，从这些前缀中获取中间答案，并利用这些前缀：1）构建额外的训练轨迹；2）通过前缀间的性能差异推导步级奖励。该方法使用单一共享模型同时进行搜索策略学习和前缀答案评估，无需额外人工标注或独立奖励模型。

Result: 在多跳问答基准测试中，PRAISE相比强基线方法持续提升了性能表现，验证了该方法在改善数据效率和信用分配方面的有效性。

Conclusion: PRAISE通过重用搜索轨迹前缀和推导中间奖励，有效解决了智能搜索训练中的数据效率低和奖励稀疏问题，为基于搜索的强化学习提供了一种无需额外标注的联合优化框架。

Abstract: In agentic search, large language models (LLMs) are trained to perform multi-turn retrieval and reasoning for complex tasks such as multi-hop question answering (QA). However, current search-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods suffer from two core limitations: expensive long-horizon rollouts are under-utilized during training, and supervision is typically available only at the final answer, resulting in severe reward sparsity. We present Prefix-based Rollout reuse for Agentic search with Intermediate Step rEwards (PRAISE), a framework for improving both data efficiency and credit assignment in agentic search training. Given a complete search trajectory, PRAISE extracts prefix states at different search turns, elicits intermediate answers from them, and uses these prefixes both to construct additional training trajectories and to derive step-level rewards from performance differences across prefixes. Our method uses a single shared model for both search policy learning and prefix answer evaluation, enabling joint optimization without extra human annotations or a separate reward model. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that PRAISE consistently improves performance over strong baselines.

</details>


### [111] [Structured Multi-Criteria Evaluation of Large Language Models with Fuzzy Analytic Hierarchy Process and DualJudge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03742)
*Yulong He,Ivan Smirnov,Dmitry Fedrushkov,Sergey Kovalchuk,Ilya Revin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将层次分析法(AHP)和模糊层次分析法(FAHP)应用于大语言模型评估，并基于此开发了DualJudge混合框架，通过结合直觉性和分析性评估范式提升评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大语言模型直接评分方法存在不一致性和不透明性问题，需要更可靠、结构化的评估框架来克服这些局限性。

Method: 1) 将层次分析法(AHP)应用于LLM评估；2) 提出置信度感知的模糊层次分析法(FAHP)，使用三角模糊数建模认知不确定性；3) 基于双过程理论提出DualJudge混合框架，通过一致性感知加权自适应融合直接评分和结构化AHP输出。

Result: 在JudgeBench上的系统验证表明：清晰和模糊AHP在不同模型规模和数据集划分上都一致优于直接评分，FAHP在不确定比较场景中表现出更好的稳定性。DualJudge框架实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的结构化推理为更可靠的LLM评估提供了原则性途径，直觉性和深思熟虑性评估范式具有互补优势，混合方法能显著提升评估质量。

Abstract: Effective evaluation of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical bottleneck, as conventional direct scoring often yields inconsistent and opaque judgments. In this work, we adapt the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP) to LLM-based evaluation and, more importantly, propose a confidence-aware Fuzzy AHP (FAHP) extension that models epistemic uncertainty via triangular fuzzy numbers modulated by LLM-generated confidence scores. Systematically validated on JudgeBench, our structured approach decomposes assessments into explicit criteria and incorporates uncertainty-aware aggregation, producing more calibrated judgments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that both crisp and fuzzy AHP consistently outperform direct scoring across model scales and dataset splits, with FAHP showing superior stability in uncertain comparison scenarios. Building on these insights, we propose \textbf{DualJudge}, a hybrid framework inspired by Dual-Process Theory that adaptively fuses holistic direct scores with structured AHP outputs via consistency-aware weighting. DualJudge achieves state-of-the-art performance, underscoring the complementary strengths of intuitive and deliberative evaluation paradigms. These results establish uncertainty-aware structured reasoning as a principled pathway toward more reliable LLM assessment. Code is available at https://github.com/hreyulog/AHP_llm_judge.

</details>


### [112] [RL-Driven Sustainable Land-Use Allocation for the Lake Malawi Basin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03768)
*Ying Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个深度强化学习框架，用于优化马拉维湖流域的土地利用分配，以最大化生态系统服务价值，通过PPO算法结合空间奖励塑造实现生态合理的土地利用模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态敏感地区的不可持续土地利用实践威胁生物多样性、水资源和数百万人的生计，需要开发智能优化方法来平衡生态保护与土地利用需求。

Method: 使用深度强化学习框架，基于Sentinel-2影像的9个土地覆盖类别，采用PPO算法在50x50网格上进行土地利用像素转移优化，奖励函数结合生态系统服务价值和空间连贯性目标。

Result: 智能体有效学习增加总生态系统服务价值；空间奖励塑造成功引导分配形成生态合理的土地利用模式；框架对政策参数变化响应良好，可作为环境规划的情景分析工具。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习框架能够有效优化土地利用分配，实现生态系统服务价值最大化，同时考虑空间生态模式，为环境规划提供了有价值的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Unsustainable land-use practices in ecologically sensitive regions threaten biodiversity, water resources, and the livelihoods of millions. This paper presents a deep reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimizing land-use allocation in the Lake Malawi Basin to maximize total ecosystem service value (ESV). Drawing on the benefit transfer methodology of Costanza et al., we assign biome-specific ESV coefficients -- locally anchored to a Malawi wetland valuation -- to nine land-cover classes derived from Sentinel-2 imagery.
